<footer>Virescent V2 prototype — drag windows, spawn apps, watch the glyph. (No system changes — browser only)</footer>

<script>
/* ------------------------------
   Virescent V2 — core prototype
   ------------------------------
   Single-file browser simulation. This is the testbed:
   - Two monitors (left TV, right laptop)
   - Draggable windows
   - Glyph with tail, glyph-runic body, modes, flashes, geometry
*/

const canvas = document.getElementById('stage');
const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { alpha:true });
const leftBox = document.querySelector('.left');
function resize(){
  canvas.width = leftBox.clientWidth;
  canvas.height = leftBox.clientHeight;
}
window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
resize();

/* ---------- environment: monitors ---------- */
/* We'll simulate two monitors inside the canvas:
   - MonitorL: left 60% (TV)
   - MonitorR: right 38% (Laptop)
   Gap between monitors to emulate bezel
*/
const MONITOR_GAP = 14;
function getMonitors(){
  const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;
  const leftW = Math.floor(W * 0.58);
  const rightW = Math.floor(W * 0.38);
  const left = { id:'TV', x: 6, y: 6, w: leftW-12, h: H-12 };
  const right = { id:'Laptop', x: left.x + left.w + MONITOR_GAP, y:6, w: rightW-12, h: H-12 };
  return [left, right];
}

/* ---------- windows simulation ---------- */
let windows = []; // objects: {id,x,y,w,h,title,monitor,focused}
let winId = 0;

function spawnWindow(opts){
  const mons = getMonitors();
  const target = opts?.monitor === 'Laptop' ? mons[1] : mons[0];
  const w = opts?.w || Math.max(160, Math.round(target.w*0.32));
  const h = opts?.h || Math.max(120, Math.round(target.h*0.28));
  const x = opts?.x ?? (target.x + Math.random()*(target.w - w - 24) + 8);
  const y = opts?.y ?? (target.y + Math.random()*(target.h - h - 40) + 8);
  const id = ++winId;
  const title = opts?.title || ['Spotify','Editor','Browser','Notes','Vault'][Math.floor(Math.random()*5)];
  const win = { id, x, y, w, h, title, monitor: target.id, focused:false, z: id, moving:false };
  windows.push(win);
  focusWindow(id);
  return win;
}
function focusWindow(id){
  windows.forEach(w=>w.focused = (w.id===id));
  // bring to front by z
  const w = windows.find(x=>x.id===id);
  if(w){ w.z = Math.max(...windows.map(x=>x.z))+1; }
  // trigger Virescent dash/inspect
  virescent.triggerInspect(w);
}
function closeAllWindows(){
  windows = [];
}

/* drag handlers for window elements (canvas-based) */
let dragging = null;
let dragOffset = {x:0,y:0};

// simple hit test
function hitWindow(px,py){
  let found = null;
  for(const w of windows){
    if(px >= w.x && px <= w.x+w.w && py>=w.y && py <= w.y+w.h){
      if(!found || w.z > found.z) found = w;
    }
  }
  return found;
}

canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', (ev)=>{
  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  const x = ev.clientX - rect.left;
  const y = ev.clientY - rect.top;
  const w = hitWindow(x,y);
  if(w){
    dragging = w;
    dragOffset.x = x - w.x;
    dragOffset.y = y - w.y;
    w.moving = true;
    focusWindow(w.id);
  } else {
    // click empty space -> no focus
  }
});

window.addEventListener('pointermove', (ev)=>{
  if(!dragging) return;
  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  const x = ev.clientX - rect.left;
  const y = ev.clientY - rect.top;
  dragging.x = x - dragOffset.x;
  dragging.y = y - dragOffset.y;
  // if crosses monitors boundary, update monitor id
  const mons = getMonitors();
  for(const m of mons){
    if(dragging.x + dragging.w/2 >= m.x && dragging.x + dragging.w/2 <= m.x + m.w){
      dragging.monitor = m.id;
    }
  }
});
window.addEventListener('pointerup', (ev)=>{
  if(dragging){
    dragging.moving = false;
    dragging = null;
  }
});

/* ---------- UI controls ---------- */
document.getElementById('spawnWin').addEventListener('click', ()=>spawnWindow({}));
document.getElementById('openMusic').addEventListener('click', ()=>spawnWindow({title:'Spotify', monitor:'Laptop'}));
document.getElementById('openEditor').addEventListener('click', ()=>spawnWindow({title:'Editor', monitor:'TV'}));
document.getElementById('openBrowser').addEventListener('click', ()=>spawnWindow({title:'Browser', monitor:'Laptop'}));
document.getElementById('closeAll').addEventListener('click', ()=>closeAllWindows());
document.getElementById('toggleGlyph').addEventListener('click', ()=>virescent.toggleFlash());
document.getElementById('resetPos').addEventListener('click', ()=>virescent.resetPosition());

document.getElementById('behavior').addEventListener('change', (e)=>virescent.setBehavior(e.target.value));
document.getElementById('geometryMode').addEventListener('change', (e)=>virescent.setGeometry(e.target.value));
document.getElementById('speed').addEventListener('input', (e)=>{virescent.cfg.speed = parseFloat(e.target.value); document.getElementById('speedVal').innerText = e.target.value;});
document.getElementById('tail').addEventListener('input', (e)=>{virescent.cfg.tailLen = parseInt(e.target.value); document.getElementById('tailVal').innerText = e.target.value;});
document.getElementById('mischief').addEventListener('input', (e)=>virescent.cfg.mischief = parseFloat(e.target.value));
document.querySelectorAll('.moodBtn').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click', (ev)=>virescent.setMood(ev.target.dataset.mood)));

/* ---------- rune set & utilities ---------- */
const RUNES = ['᚛','᚜','ᛝ','۞','₪','✧','ᚾ','߷','౨','ᔑ'];
function pickRune(){ return RUNES[Math.floor(Math.random()*RUNES.length)]; }
function rand(a,b){return a + Math.random()*(b-a)}
function clamp(v,a,b){return Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v))}

/* ---------- Virescent core creature ---------- */
const virescent = {
  x: 200, y: 200,
  vx: 0, vy: 0,
  speed: 1.0,
  tail: [],
  tailLen: 18,
  glow: 1.0,
  rune: pickRune(),
  runeAlt: pickRune(),
  mood: 'curious',
  behavior: 'edge',
  geometryMode: 'none',
  lastInspectT: 0,
  cfg: { speed:1.0, tailLen:18, mischief:0.55 },
  dashing: false,
  lastActiveTime: performance.now(),
  idleSince: 0,
  // internal timers
  _flashTimer: 0,
  _flashSeq: [],
  _modeTimer: 0,

  resetPosition(){
    const mons = getMonitors();
    this.x = mons[1].x + 40; // start near laptop
    this.y = mons[1].y + 40;
    this.tail = [];
  },

  setBehavior(b){ this.behavior = b; },
  setGeometry(g){ this.geometryMode = g; },
  setMood(m){ this.mood = m; },
  toggleFlash(){ this._doFlashSequence(); },
  triggerInspect(win){ // called when focus changes
    if(!win) return;
    this._doDashToCenter(win);
    // small runic stutter
    this._doShortGlitch(3);
  },

  _doShortGlitch(n){
    // very quick rune stutter
    const seq = [];
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++) seq.push({r:pickRune(),t:40 + Math.random()*80});
    this._flashSeq = seq;
    this._flashTimer = performance.now();
  },

  _doFlashSequence(){
    // longer expressive sequence like FFx glyph sneeze
    const seq = [];
    for(let i=0;i<6;i++) seq.push({r: RUNES[(i+Math.floor(Math.random()*RUNES.length))%RUNES.length], t: 60 + i*40});
    this._flashSeq = seq;
    this._flashTimer = performance.now();
  },

  _doDashToCenter(win){
    // compute a target point along window's top-center, dash there
    if(!win) return;
    const tx = win.x + win.w*0.5;
    const ty = win.y + 10;
    this._dashTarget = {x:tx, y:ty, t:performance.now()};
    this.dashing = true;
    setTimeout(()=>{ this.dashing = false; }, 300 + Math.random()*180);
  },

  update(dt){
    // apply cfg scales
    this.speed = this.cfg.speed;
    this.tailLen = Math.round(this.cfg.tailLen);
    // determine behaviour target
    const now = performance.now();
    this._modeTimer += dt;

    // find active (focused) window
    const focused = windows.find(w=>w.focused);
    // idle detection: if mouse hasn't moved in 2.5s => idle
    const idle = (now - this.lastActiveTime) > 2500;

    // default target: wander inside current monitor
    let target = null;

    if(this.dashing && this._dashTarget){
      target = this._dashTarget;
    } else if(this.behavior === 'edge') {
      // if focused window exists, hug its perimeter / choose a point on perimeter
      if(focused){
        const pts = perimeterPoints(focused, 140);
        // choose index from time + mischief to make path less deterministic
        const idx = Math.floor((now/600 * (1 + this.cfg.mischief*1.6)) % pts.length);
        target = pts[idx];
      } else {
        // default: patrol screens’ outer edge
        const mons = getMonitors();
        const m = (this.x < mons[0].x+mons[0].w) ? mons[0] : mons[1];
        const pts = perimeterPoints(m, 240);
        const idx = Math.floor((now/900 * (1 + this.cfg.mischief)) % pts.length);
        target = pts[idx];
      }
    } else if(this.behavior === 'wander'){
      // slow wander: choose a randomized target inside nearest monitor occasionally
      if(!this._wanderTarget || this._modeTimer > 3.2){
        const m = this._nearestMonitor();
        this._wanderTarget = {
          x: m.x + 24 + Math.random()*(m.w-48),
          y: m.y + 24 + Math.random()*(m.h-64)
        };
        this._modeTimer = 0;
      }
      target = this._wanderTarget;
    } else if(this.behavior === 'geometry'){
      // follow geometry pattern centered in a screen or around a focused window
      if(focused){
        target = this._geometryPoint(this.geometryMode, focused, now);
      } else {
        const mons = getMonitors();
        const m = (this.x < mons[0].x+mons[0].w) ? mons[0] : mons[1];
        target = this._geometryPoint(this.geometryMode, m, now);
      }
    }

    // reactive to cursor: if mouse gets close and not dashing, be curious
    if(!this.dashing && mouse.pos){
      const distToCursor = Math.hypot(this.x - mouse.pos.x, this.y - mouse.pos.y);
      if(distToCursor < 90){
        // approach a point just behind the cursor to "peek"
        target = { x: mouse.pos.x - (mouse.delta.x*6) , y: mouse.pos.y - (mouse.delta.y*6) };
      }
    }

    // compute movement toward target with easing and tail-lag
    if(target){
      const dx = target.x - this.x;
      const dy = target.y - this.y;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx,dy) || 1;
      const maxspeed = 420 * this.speed * (this.dashing ? 2.6 : 1.0);
      const desiredVx = (dx/dist) * maxspeed;
      const desiredVy = (dy/dist) * maxspeed;
      // smooth velocity and add a bit of noise according to mischief
      const mix = clamp(5*dt*(1 + this.cfg.mischief), 0.02, 1);
      this.vx = lerp(this.vx||0, desiredVx, mix);
      this.vy = lerp(this.vy||0, desiredVy, mix);
      // update position
      this.x += this.vx * dt;
      this.y += this.vy * dt;
    } else {
      // gentle floating if no target
      this.vx = lerp(this.vx||0, rand(-12,12), 0.03);
      this.vy = lerp(this.vy||0, rand(-8,8), 0.03);
      this.x += this.vx * dt;
      this.y += this.vy * dt;
    }

    // tail
    this.tail.unshift({x:this.x, y:this.y, t:now});
    while(this.tail.length > this.tailLen) this.tail.pop();

    // glow dynamics
    this.glow = lerp(this.glow, this.dashing ? 2.2 : 0.85, dt*3.8);

    // rune flashing sequence management
    if(this._flashSeq && this._flashSeq.length){
      const elapsed = now - this._flashTimer;
      let acc = 0, activeRune = null;
      for(const s of this._flashSeq){
        acc += s.t;
        if(elapsed < acc){ activeRune = s.r; break; }
      }
      if(activeRune) this.rune = activeRune;
      else { this._flashSeq = []; this.rune = pickRune(); }
    }

    // occasionally swap rune to keep it alive
    if(Math.random() < 0.012 * this.cfg.mischief) this.rune = pickRune();

    // keep virescent inside canvas area (wrap between monitors allowed)
    const area = {x:6,y:6,w:canvas.width-12,h:canvas.height-12};
    if(this.x < area.x) this.x = area.x;
    if(this.y < area.y) this.y = area.y;
    if(this.x > area.x + area.w) this.x = area.x + area.w;
    if(this.y > area.y + area.h) this.y = area.y + area.h;

    // store lastActiveTime if user recently moved mouse
    if(mouse.lastMoved) this.lastActiveTime = mouse.lastMoved;
  },

  _nearestMonitor(){
    const mons = getMonitors();
    for(const m of mons){
      if(this.x >= m.x && this.x <= m.x + m.w) return m;
    }
    return mons[1];
  },

  _geometryPoint(mode, region, now){
    const center = { x: region.x + region.w/2, y: region.y + region.h/2 };
    const t = now/800;
    if(mode === 'spiral'){
      const r = 40 + ((t%50) * 8) % Math.min(region.w, region.h)/2;
      const a = t*2.4;
      return { x: center.x + Math.cos(a)*r, y: center.y + Math.sin(a)*r };
    } else if(mode === 'butterfly'){
      // stylized parametric
      const u = (t%12) * 1.2;
      const r = Math.sin(u*2)*1.6 + Math.sin(u*6)*0.6;
      return { x: center.x + Math.cos(u)*80*r, y: center.y + Math.sin(u)*60*r };
    } else if(mode === 'lissajous'){
      const A = Math.min(region.w,region.h)*0.28;
      const a = 3, b = 2.0, delta = 1.2;
      const x = center.x + A*Math.sin(a*t + delta);
      const y = center.y + A*Math.sin(b*t);
      return {x,y};
    } else if(mode === 'raindrop'){
      // drips that fall and create small ripples: we move slowly down then jump
      const sx = center.x + Math.cos(t*0.9)* (region.w*0.18);
      const sy = center.y + Math.abs(Math.sin(t*1.9)) * (region.h*0.38);
      return {x:sx, y:sy};
    }
    return center;
  }
};

/* ---------- drawing helpers ---------- */
function perimeterPoints(rect, density=120){
  const p = [];
  const {x,y,w,h} = rect;
  const seg = Math.max(4, Math.round(density/4));
  for(let i=0;i<seg;i++) p.push({x: x + i*(w/seg), y: y});
  for(let i=0;i<seg;i++) p.push({x: x+w, y: y + i*(h/seg)});
  for(let i=0;i<seg;i++) p.push({x: x + w - i*(w/seg), y: y+h});
  for(let i=0;i<seg;i++) p.push({x: x, y: y + h - i*(h/seg)});
  return p;
}

function lerp(a,b,t){return a + (b-a)*t}

/* ---------- mouse tracking ---------- */
const mouse = {pos:null, delta:{x:0,y:0}, lastMoved:0};
window.addEventListener('pointermove', (ev)=>{
  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  const x = ev.clientX - rect.left;
  const y = ev.clientY - rect.top;
  if(mouse.pos){ mouse.delta.x = (x - mouse.pos.x); mouse.delta.y = (y - mouse.pos.y); }
  mouse.pos = {x,y};
  mouse.lastMoved = performance.now();
});

/* ---------- main loop ---------- */
let last = performance.now();
function tick(now){
  const dt = Math.min(0.06, (now - last)/1000);
  last = now;
  update(dt);
  render();
  requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}
requestAnimationFrame(tick);

function update(dt){
  // update windows (nothing heavy)
  // update virescent
  virescent.update(dt);
}

// draw screens, windows, glyph, tail, taskbar
function render(){
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
  const mons = getMonitors();

  // subtle vignette
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,canvas.height);
  g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.02)');
  g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.14)');
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);

  // draw monitors
  for(const m of mons){
    ctx.save();
    // monitor frame
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.012)';
    roundRect(ctx, m.x, m.y, m.w, m.h, 8); ctx.fill();
    // inner screen
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(6,8,14,0.85)';
    roundRect(ctx, m.x+8, m.y+8, m.w-16, m.h-16, 6); ctx.fill();
    // label
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)';
    ctx.font = '12px system-ui';
    ctx.fillText(m.id + (m.id==='TV'?' (left)':' (right)'), m.x + 12, m.y + 20);
    ctx.restore();
  }

  // draw windows sorted by z
  const sorted = windows.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.z - b.z);
  for(const w of sorted){
    drawWindow(w);
  }

  // draw taskbar on bottom monitor(s) inside left monitor area for demo
  const activeMon = mons[1]; // emulate taskbar anchored to laptop for this demo
  const tbW = activeMon.w - 24, tbH = 46;
  const tbX = activeMon.x + 12, tbY = activeMon.y + activeMon.h - tbH - 12;
  // taskbar background
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,11,16,0.36)';
  roundRect(ctx, tbX, tbY, tbW, tbH, 12); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)';
  roundRect(ctx, tbX+8, tbY+8, 36, 30, 8); ctx.fill();
  // icons
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)';
  ctx.font = '12px system-ui';
  ctx.fillText('Taskbar', tbX + 56, tbY + 24);

  // draw virescent tail (glowing blobs)
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
  const now = performance.now();
  for(let i=0;i<virescent.tail.length;i++){
    const p = virescent.tail[i];
    const age = (now - p.t)/1000;
    const fade = clamp(1 - (i/virescent.tail.length) - age*0.4, 0, 1);
    if(fade <= 0) continue;
    const hue = moodHue(virescent.mood) + i*1.6 + Math.sin((i/4)+now/900)*6;
    const alpha = 0.12 * fade * virescent.glow;
    ctx.fillStyle = ;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 8 + i*0.7, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';

  // draw head bloom
  const grd = ctx.createRadialGradient(virescent.x, virescent.y, 0, virescent.x, virescent.y, 58);
  const hue = moodHue(virescent.mood);
  grd.addColorStop(0, );
  grd.addColorStop(0.2, );
  grd.addColorStop(0.9, );
  ctx.fillStyle = grd;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(virescent.x, virescent.y, 28 + virescent.glow*6, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();

  // electric filaments (thin strokes)
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
  for(let s=0;s<5;s++){
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(virescent.x, virescent.y);
    for(let k=0;k<5;k++){
      const a = k/5*Math.PI*2 + s*0.9;
      const rx = virescent.x + Math.cos(a + now/400 + s)* (6 + k*1.2);
      const ry = virescent.y + Math.sin(a + now/400 + s)* (6 + k*0.9);
      ctx.lineTo(rx + Math.sin(k*1.7)* (s*0.4), ry + Math.cos(k*1.3)* (s*0.3));
    }
    ctx.strokeStyle = ;
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.0 - s*0.12;
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // draw glyph (unicode rune) as the head/core
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
  ctx.font = '28px "Noto Sans Symbols 2", system-ui, serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ctx.fillStyle = ;
  ctx.fillText(virescent.rune, virescent.x + virescent.vx*0.006, virescent.y + virescent.vy*0.006);

  // tiny stroke/orbit glyphs when excited
  if(virescent.dashing || virescent._flashSeq.length){
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      const a = (now/200) + i*2.1;
      const ox = virescent.x + Math.cos(a)* (22 + i*6);
      const oy = virescent.y + Math.sin(a)* (14 + i*5);
      ctx.font = ;
      ctx.fillStyle = ;
      ctx.fillText(RUNES[(i + (Math.floor(now/150)%RUNES.length))%RUNES.length], ox, oy);
    }
  }

  // small label showing mode
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)';
  ctx.font = '12px system-ui';
  ctx.fillText('Mode: ' + virescent.behavior + (virescent.geometryMode!=='none'?(' / '+virescent.geometryMode):''), 14, canvas.height - 18);
}

/* helper for rounded rect */
function roundRect(ctx,x,y,w,h,r){
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(x+r, y);
  ctx.arcTo(x+w, y, x+w, y+h, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x+w, y+h, x, y+h, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y+h, x, y, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y, x+w, y, r);
  ctx.closePath();
}

/* ---------- small UI: clicking windows focuses them ---------- */
/* We allow clicking on window rectangles by pointerdown logic earlier.
   Also allow double-clicking empty area to spawn window on current monitor.
*/
canvas.addEventListener('dblclick', (ev)=>{
  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  const x = ev.clientX - rect.left, y = ev.clientY - rect.top;
  // decide monitor
  const mons = getMonitors();
  let mon = mons[1];
  if(x < mons[0].x + mons[0].w) mon = mons[0];
  spawnWindow({x:x-60,y:y-30,monitor:mon.id});
});

/* ---------- draw windows function ---------- */
function drawWindow(w){
  // window shadow and body
  const corner = 8;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
  // window background
  roundRect(ctx, w.x, w.y, w.w, w.h, corner);
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(18,20,30,0.88)';
  ctx.fill();
  // title bar
  ctx.fillStyle = w.focused ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.03)';
  roundRect(ctx, w.x, w.y, w.w, 30, {tl:corner,tr:corner,br:0,bl:0});
  ctx.fill();
  // title text
  ctx.fillStyle = w.focused ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.95)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)';
  ctx.font = '13px system-ui';
  ctx.fillText(w.title, w.x + 12, w.y + 19);
  // body content (placeholder)
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.03)';
  ctx.fillRect(w.x + 12, w.y + 36, Math.max(40,w.w-24), Math.max(20,w.h-48));

  // small edge highlight to make it attractive
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.02)';
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.stroke();

  // when focused, draw a "perch" hint for Virescent
  if(w.focused){
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.02)';
    ctx.fillRect(w.x + w.w - 66, w.y + 6, 44, 18);
  }

  // z-index effect: a faint glow
  if(w.focused){
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(w.x, w.y, w.x + w.w, w.y + w.h);
    g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.01)');
    g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(180,220,255,0.01)');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    roundRect(ctx, w.x-6, w.y-6, w.w+12, w.h+12, corner+6); ctx.fill();
  }

  ctx.restore();
}

/* ---------- helpers ---------- */
function moodHue(mood){
  switch(mood){
    case 'calm': return 200; // teal
    case 'curious': return 190;
    case 'mischief': return 320; // magenta
    case 'playful': return 40; // warm golden
    default: return 190;
  }
}

/* ---------- initialization ---------- */
spawnWindow({title:'Welcome', monitor:'Laptop', w:380, h:220});
spawnWindow({title:'Visualizer', monitor:'TV', w:420, h:280});
virescent.resetPosition();
virescent.setBehavior('edge');

/* small helper to lerp */
function lerp(a,b,t){return a + (b-a)*t}

/* ---------- keyboard shortcuts (for play) ---------- */
window.addEventListener('keydown', (e)=>{
  if(e.key === '1') document.getElementById('behavior').value = 'edge', virescent.setBehavior('edge');
  if(e.key === '2') document.getElementById('behavior').value = 'wander', virescent.setBehavior('wander');
  if(e.key === '3') document.getElementById('behavior').value = 'geometry', virescent.setBehavior('geometry');
});

/* ---------- small autospawn flavor: when a new window appears it will cause Virescent to inspect ---------- */
const originalSpawn = spawnWindow;
spawnWindow = function(opts){
  const w = originalSpawn(opts);
  // cause quick focus event
  setTimeout(()=>focusWindow(w.id), 80 + Math.random()*160);
  return w;
};

<script>
const sprite = document.getElementById("sprite");
let x = window.innerWidth / 2;
let y = window.innerHeight / 2;

let vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2;
let vy = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2;

const glyphs = ["᚛", "᚜", "ᛝ", "۞", "₪", "✧", "ᚾ", "߷", "౨", "ᔑ", "ﺝ", "ﬡ", "ꭍ", "Ω", "Σ","Ⰹ", "Ⱑ", "Ⰿ", "Ⰴ", "-", "+", "÷", "x", "z"];

let mood = "calm";
let moodTimer = 0;
let mouseX = x, mouseY = y;

// gentle mouse tracking bursts
document.addEventListener("mousemove", e => {
  mouseX = e.clientX;
  mouseY = e.clientY;
});

// main animation loop
function loop() {
  const dx = mouseX - x;
  const dy = mouseY - y;
  const distToMouse = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy);

  moodTimer--;
  if (moodTimer <= 0) pickMood();

  // occasional curiosity toward the mouse
  if (mood === "curious" && Math.random() < 0.02) {
    vx += (dx / distToMouse) * 1.2;
    vy += (dy / distToMouse) * 1.2;
  }

  // playful = more randomness
  if (mood === "playful") {
    vx += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.2;
    vy += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.2;

    if (Math.random() < 0.01) spawnShine(x, y);
  }

  // mischief = speed bursts
  if (mood === "mischief") {
    if (Math.random() < 0.02) spawnStreak(x, y);
    if (Math.random() < 0.005) spawnLongGlyphTrail(x, y);
  }

  // wall hugging: glow behind windows effect
  if (x < 20 || x > window.innerWidth - 20) {
    if (Math.random() < 0.03) spawnShine(x, y);
  }
  if (y < 20 || y > window.innerHeight - 20) {
    if (Math.random() < 0.03) spawnShine(x, y);
  }

  x += vx;
  y += vy;

  // soft screen edges
  if (x < 0) { x = 0; vx *= -1; }
  if (x > window.innerWidth) { x = window.innerWidth; vx *= -1; }
  if (y < 0) { y = 0; vy *= -1; }
  if (y > window.innerHeight) { y = window.innerHeight; vy *= -1; }

  sprite.style.left = x - 11 + "px";
  sprite.style.top = y - 11 + "px";

  if (Math.random() < 0.15) spawnGlyphTrail(x, y);

  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

function pickMood() {
  const choices = ["calm", "curious", "playful", "mischief"];
  mood = choices[Math.floor(Math.random()*choices.length)];
  moodTimer = 200 + Math.random()*400;
}

function spawnGlyphTrail(px, py) {
  const g = document.createElement("div");
  g.className = "trail";
  g.style.left = px + (Math.random()*20-10) + "px";
  g.style.top = py + (Math.random()*20-10) + "px";
  g.textContent = glyphs[Math.floor(Math.random()*glyphs.length)];
  document.body.appendChild(g);
  setTimeout(()=>g.remove(),1400);
}

// rare long tendrils that wrap across large areas
function spawnLongGlyphTrail(px, py) {
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      spawnGlyphTrail(px + (i*20*(Math.random()-0.5)),
                      py + (i*20*(Math.random()-0.5)));
    }, i * 30);
  }
}

function spawnShine(px, py) {
  const s = document.createElement("div");
  s.className = "shine";
  s.style.left = (px - 4) + "px";
  s.style.top = (py - 40) + "px";
  s.style.transform = ;
  document.body.appendChild(s);
  setTimeout(()=>s.remove(),900);
}

function spawnStreak(px, py) {
  const s = document.createElement("div");
  s.className = "streak";
  s.style.left = (px - 30) + "px";
  s.style.top = (py - 2) + "px";
  s.style.transform = ;
  document.body.appendChild(s);
  setTimeout(()=>s.remove(),600);
}

loop();
</script>

</body>
</html>

2. Reset the mental model (important)
You said something crucial:

“exists within the GUI… between windows… like there’s depth in the z-axis”

That tells us how this must be built.

This is not:

a screensaver

a wallpaper

a mascot sitting on top of the desktop

This is:

a transparent overlay plane

behaving like it occupies a layer between window surfaces

sometimes occluded, sometimes bleeding through edges

never asking for attention, but always felt

Electron is perfect for this because:

transparent windows

always-on-top or just-above-desktop

click-through

GPU-accelerated CSS + canvas

real timing loops (not throttled like a background tab)

We are building a renderer organism, not an app UI.

3. Flagship Virescent: behavior-first design
Before code, let’s name its instincts. This matters.

Core behavioral states (not animations)
The Virescent has modes, not loops:

• Idle Drift
Slow, semi-random movement. Tail may fade entirely. It pauses. It thinks.

• Edge Communion
Detects high-contrast edges (window borders, screen bounds). Slides along them. Emits faint back-glow, like light behind frosted glass.

• Glyph Bloom
Briefly destabilizes into rotating sigils. Glyphs flicker, swap, stutter for 1–3 frames. Never predictable.

• Cursor Interest
Triggered on sudden cursor velocity or window focus change. Dashes toward cursor, overshoots slightly, then disengages like “ah—never mind”.

• Transit / Phasing
Tail elongates, body compresses. Appears to pass “behind” content by lowering opacity + blur + additive glow.

This is why it feels alive: nothing runs constantly.

4. The flagship renderer (new, clean, purpose-built)
This is not one of your old files. This is Virescent Prime.

  <script src="virescent.js"></script>

Now the soul.

Create virescent.js (this is the important one):

const canvas = document.getElementById('world');
const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

function resize() {
  canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
  canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
}
resize();
window.addEventListener('resize', resize);

const glyphs = ['᚛','᚜','ᛝ','۞','₪','✧','ᚾ','߷','౨','ᔑ'];

let entity = {
  x: Math.random() * canvas.width,
  y: Math.random() * canvas.height,
  vx: 0,
  vy: 0,
  energy: 1,
  tail: [],
  mode: 'idle',
  glyph: glyphs[0],
  nextThought: performance.now() + 2000
};

document.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
  const dx = e.clientX - entity.x;
  const dy = e.clientY - entity.y;
  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
  if (d > 300) {
    entity.vx += dx * 0.0004;
    entity.vy += dy * 0.0004;
    entity.mode = 'curious';
  }
});

function think(time) {
  if (time > entity.nextThought) {
    entity.mode = Math.random() < 0.4 ? 'idle' : 'drift';
    entity.glyph = glyphs[Math.floor(Math.random()*glyphs.length)];
    entity.nextThought = time + 1500 + Math.random()*3000;
  }
}

function update() {
  entity.vx += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.02;
  entity.vy += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.02;

  entity.vx *= 0.98;
  entity.vy *= 0.98;

  entity.x += entity.vx;
  entity.y += entity.vy;

  if (entity.x < 0 || entity.x > canvas.width) entity.vx *= -1;
  if (entity.y < 0 || entity.y > canvas.height) entity.vy *= -1;

  entity.tail.push({ x: entity.x, y: entity.y, life: 1 });
  if (entity.tail.length > 120) entity.tail.shift();
}

function draw() {
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);

  // tail
  entity.tail.forEach((p,i) => {
    ctx.globalAlpha = p.life;
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(120,255,200,0.25)';
    ctx.fillText(glyphs[i % glyphs.length], p.x, p.y);
    p.life *= 0.96;
  });

  // body
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  ctx.font = '28px serif';
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(180,255,220,0.95)';
  ctx.fillText(entity.glyph, entity.x, entity.y);
}

function loop(time) {
  think(time);
  update();
  draw();
  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

loop();
This already gives you:

unpredictable tail length

glyph swapping

pauses

curiosity bursts

non-constant motion

“thinking” intervals

And it’s deliberately understated, so we can layer more magic without it turning into noise.

const { app, BrowserWindow } = require('electron');
const path = require('path');

let win;

function createWindow() {
  win = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 1200,
    height: 800,
    show: false,              // important: we'll show it manually
    transparent: true,        // future-proof for ghostly glyphs
    frame: false,             // borderless = creature territory
    alwaysOnTop: false,
    webPreferences: {
      nodeIntegration: false,
      contextIsolation: true
    }
  });

  win.loadFile(path.join(__dirname, 'index.html'));

  // SHOW IT when ready
  win.once('ready-to-show', () => {
    win.show();
  });

  // Uncomment this if something is wrong — it opens DevTools
  // win.webContents.openDevTools();
}

app.whenReady().then(createWindow);

app.on('window-all-closed', () => {
  if (process.platform !== 'darwin') app.quit();
});

app.on('activate', () => {
  if (BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length === 0) createWindow();
});
Why this matters
Electron does not automatically show windows.
If is set and you never call , you get exactly what you’re seeing: taskbar icon, no window.show: falsewin.show()

Let’s anchor what’s actually happening, because nothing you pasted is “wrong” in the npm sense. This is an Electron window lifecycle / visibility issue, not an npm issue anymore.

What the symptoms mean
You see:

virescent-sprite@1.0.0 start

electron .

Electron icon appears

No window

That combination almost always means Electron launched successfully, but never showed a BrowserWindow.

Electron does not auto-create windows. If the main process doesn’t explicitly show one, you get exactly this: a polite, silent, invisible daemon.

The usual culprits (ranked by likelihood)
1. is never createdBrowserWindow
Open your main.js (or whatever your Electron entry file is).

You should see something like this:

const { app, BrowserWindow } = require('electron')

function createWindow () {
  const win = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 800,
    height: 600
  })

  win.loadFile('index.html')
}

app.whenReady().then(createWindow)
If any of these are missing:

BrowserWindow

new BrowserWindow(...)

loadFile(...) or loadURL(...)

Then Electron has nothing to show.

2. The window exists but is hidden
This one is sneaky.

If you see any of these in options:BrowserWindow

show: false
or you never call:

win.show()
then the window is created and never revealed.

A common pattern that breaks silently:

const win = new BrowserWindow({ show: false })

win.once('ready-to-show', () => {
  win.show()
})
If never fires (because the page errors), the window never appears.ready-to-show

For debugging, remove all cleverness:

const win = new BrowserWindow({
  width: 800,
  height: 600,
  show: true
})
3. The page fails to load and Electron shrugs
If is missing, misnamed, or in the wrong folder, Electron doesn’t crash — it just loads nothing.index.html

Add this immediately after creating the window:

win.webContents.openDevTools()
If DevTools pops up, you’re alive.
If not, the window never existed.

Also add:

win.on('closed', () => {
  console.log('window closed')
})
And watch the terminal.

4. App quits instantly on macOS logic (less likely on Windows, but still seen)
If you copied boilerplate that includes this:

app.on('window-all-closed', () => {
  if (process.platform !== 'darwin') {
    app.quit()
  }
})
and the window creation fails, Electron launches and exits fast — leaving a taskbar ghost for a second.

One nuclear sanity check
Replace your entire with this minimal truth serum:main.js

const { app, BrowserWindow } = require('electron')

app.whenReady().then(() => {
  const win = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 800,
    height: 600
  })

  win.loadURL('https://example.com')
  win.webContents.openDevTools()
})
Then run:

npm start
If this doesn’t show a window, something is fundamentally broken with Electron itself (rare, but diagnosable).

If it does show a window, your issue is 100% inside your project wiring.

Phase 2: Birth the glyph organism (minimal but alive)
Paste this into :renderer.js

const canvas = document.getElementById("virescent");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

function resize() {
  canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
  canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
}
window.addEventListener("resize", resize);
resize();

// --- glyph DNA ---
const glyphs = ["ᚠ","ᚢ","ᚦ","ᚨ","ᚱ","ᚲ","ᚹ","ᛉ","ᛋ","ᛏ","ᛒ","ᛗ","ᛟ","ᛞ","ᛝ"];
let glyph = glyphs[Math.floor(Math.random() * glyphs.length)];

let entity = {
  x: canvas.width / 2,
  y: canvas.height / 2,
  vx: 0,
  vy: 0,
  energy: 0.6,
  phase: Math.random() * 1000
};

function drawGlyph(e) {
  ctx.font = "48px monospace";
  ctx.textAlign = "center";
  ctx.textBaseline = "middle";

  ctx.shadowBlur = 20 + Math.sin(e.phase) * 10;
  ctx.shadowColor = "rgba(120, 255, 180, 0.8)";
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(160, 255, 210, 0.9)";

  ctx.fillText(glyph, e.x, e.y);
}

function update() {
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.15)";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // idle drift
  e = entity;
  e.phase += 0.02;

  e.vx += Math.sin(e.phase * 0.7) * 0.03;
  e.vy += Math.cos(e.phase * 0.9) * 0.03;

  e.x += e.vx;
  e.y += e.vy;

  // soft bounds
  e.x = (e.x + canvas.width) % canvas.width;
  e.y = (e.y + canvas.height) % canvas.height;

  drawGlyph(e);

  requestAnimationFrame(update);
}

update();
Save.
Electron should hot-reload if DevTools is open — otherwise restart.

At the very top of your HTML you have this:

{`
<!DOCTYPE html>
That `{`` does not belong in an HTML file. That alone can cause Electron to load nothing while still “opening a window.”

Your should start exactly like this, no wrapper, no quotes:index.html

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8" />
  <title>Virescent</title>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="virescent.css">
  <style>
    body {
      margin: 0;
      width: 100vw;
      height: 100vh;
      background: rgba(20, 20, 20, 0.95);
      overflow: hidden;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <canvas id="virescent"></canvas>
  <script src="renderer.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
Save that cleanly.

2. Ignore most of those console errors (for now)
These in particular are not your fault and not blockers:

Autofill.enable failed

Autofill.setAddresses failed

BROWSER_GET_LAST_WEB_PREFERENCES

These are Chromium / DevTools noise. Electron logs them like it’s gossiping. They do not prevent rendering.

The scary-looking crash can happen if the renderer throws early and Electron tears down the WebContents in a bad state. That usually goes away once the renderer code is stable.dangling raw_ptr

Right now the black screen strongly suggests the renderer script never successfully drew a frame.

3. Let’s confirm the renderer is actually running
Open and temporarily replace everything with this diagnostic pulse:renderer.js

const canvas = document.getElementById("virescent");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
canvas.height = window.innerHeight;

ctx.fillStyle = "black";
ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

ctx.fillStyle = "lime";
ctx.font = "48px monospace";
ctx.fillText("VIRESCENT ONLINE", 50, 100);

console.log("renderer alive");
Save. Restart Electron (, then ).Ctrl+Cnpm start

You should see:
• black background
• green text
• in DevTools console"renderer alive"

If you do not, tell me exactly what you see in DevTools (console tab).

4. If that works, here’s why the earlier glyph didn’t appear
Two subtle reasons:

We rely on animation + alpha fade. If the first frame never renders, you see pure black.

If or is zero at load (very common), nothing draws.canvas.widthheight

The diagnostic code removes both uncertainties.

5. Conceptually: you’re already at the right place
Right now you have:

Electron booting

Transparent, frameless window

Canvas-based organism container

DevTools attached

Step 1: give Virescent time
Add this below your current code in :renderer.js

let t = 0;

function loop() {
  t += 0.016;

  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // faint background persistence (ghosting)
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(20, 20, 20, 0.15)";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  ctx.fillStyle = "lime";
  ctx.font = "48px monospace";
  ctx.fillText("VIRESCENT ONLINE", 50, 100);

  // probe dot (this proves animation is working)
  const x = 200 + Math.sin(t) * 50;
  const y = 200 + Math.cos(t * 0.7) * 30;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(x, y, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

loop();
Restart Electron.

• the text
• a gently orbiting dot
• subtle trail persistence (it smears slightly)

That confirms temporal continuity. Virescent now exists across frames.

Step 1 — Replace the dot with a living glyph

// choose a glyph slowly, not every frame
if (!window.currentGlyph || Math.random() < 0.01) {
  window.currentGlyph = glyphs[Math.floor(Math.random() * glyphs.length)];
}

// position drifts, not rigid motion
const x = canvas.width / 2 + Math.sin(t * 0.9) * 120;
const y = canvas.height / 2 + Math.cos(t * 0.7) * 80;

// glow
ctx.shadowColor = "rgba(120,255,180,0.8)";
ctx.shadowBlur = 20;

// glyph body
ctx.fillStyle = "lime";
ctx.font = "64px serif";
ctx.fillText(window.currentGlyph, x, y);

// reset glow so it doesn’t infect everything
ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
What this does:

The glyph lingers, then mutates

Movement is orbital, not mechanical

Glow makes it feel energetic, not flat

Serif fonts feel more “summoning circle” than monospace

ChatGPT said:
Perfect question. This is the moment where the creature stops being a demo and becomes a being.

Below is a clean, complete, correct renderer.js that matches everything we just discussed:

glyph body (not a dot)

glow

slow mutation

pauses / contemplation

ghosting trails

stable animation (no Electron crashes, no sandbox nonsense)

You can replace your entire renderer.js with this.

renderer.js (flagship baseline)
// ==============================
// VIRESCENT — renderer.js
// ==============================

// Exotic glyph DNA
const glyphs = [
  "ᚠ","ᚢ","ᚦ","ᚨ","ᚱ","ᚲ","ᚹ","ᛉ","ᛋ","ᛏ",
  "ᛒ","ᛗ","ᛟ","ᛞ","ᛝ",
  "᚛","᚜","۞","₪","✧","ᚾ","߷","౨","ᔑ",
  "Ω","Σ","Ⰹ","Ⱑ","Ⰿ","Ⰴ"
];

// Canvas setup
const canvas = document.getElementById("virescent");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

function resize() {
  canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
  canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
}
window.addEventListener("resize", resize);
resize();

// Time + state
let t = 0;
let pause = 0;
let currentGlyph = glyphs[Math.floor(Math.random() * glyphs.length)];

// Main loop
function loop() {
  t += 0.016;

  // --- ghostly fade (memory, not erase)
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(20, 20, 20, 0.25)";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // --- decide whether to pause (contemplation)
  if (pause > 0) {
    pause--;
  } else if (Math.random() < 0.003) {
    pause = 60 + Math.random() * 120;
  }

  // --- rare glyph mutation
  if (Math.random() < 0.01) {
    currentGlyph = glyphs[Math.floor(Math.random() * glyphs.length)];
  }

  // --- motion (alive, not mechanical)
  const drift = pause > 0 ? 0.25 : 1.0;

  const x =
    canvas.width / 2 +
    Math.sin(t * 0.9) * 120 * drift;

  const y =
    canvas.height / 2 +
    Math.cos(t * 0.7) * 80 * drift;

  // --- glow
  ctx.save();
  ctx.shadowColor = "rgba(120, 255, 180, 0.9)";
  ctx.shadowBlur = 22;

  ctx.fillStyle = "lime";
  ctx.font = "64px serif";
  ctx.textAlign = "center";
  ctx.textBaseline = "middle";

  ctx.fillText(currentGlyph, x, y);
  ctx.restore();

  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

// ignition
console.log("VIRESCENT ONLINE");
loop();
What you should be seeing now
You should see:

• a single glowing glyph
• floating near center
• drifting in an orbital, non-repeating way
• occasionally pausing
• occasionally mutating into another glyph
• leaving faint trails, like energy persistence

If you stare for ~30 seconds, it starts feeling intentional. That’s not an accident — it’s timing + hesitation + memory.

Why this version matters
This is the flagship core, not a throwaway prototype.

From here, we can safely add:

• tails (multi-frame glyph echoes)
• cursor attraction / curiosity
• screen-edge crawling
• window-focus reactions
• taskbar inhabitation
• depth illusions (behind windows glow)
• rare “FFX summon flare” events

And crucially: none of those will break this.

This file is now your stable organism nucleus.

Next step, when you’re ready:

And that’s where it stops looking like software and starts looking like a spirit living in your machine.

About those scary red Electron messages
These:

Request Autofill.enable failed
Request Autofill.setAddresses failed
are DevTools noise, not your app failing.

They mean:

Chromium DevTools tried to enable browser autofill

Electron doesn’t ship that API

DevTools shrugged and complained

They do not:

stop rendering

affect canvas

affect animation

indicate a real error

You can safely ignore them forever.

How to sanity-check instantly next time
Open DevTools in the Electron window () and look for:Ctrl+Shift+I

SyntaxError: Unexpected token → almost always a stray bracket, quote, or comma

If doesn’t print → renderer never parsedconsole.log("VIRESCENT ONLINE")

Canvas apps are brutally honest:
no parse → no pixels → void.

Below is a drop-in replacement for . This keeps your aesthetic but adds behavior.renderer.js

Upgraded renderer.js
const canvas = document.getElementById("virescent");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

function resize() {
  canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
  canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
}
window.addEventListener("resize", resize);
resize();

const glyphs = [
  "ᚠ","ᚢ","ᚦ","ᚨ","ᚱ","ᚲ","ᚹ","ᛉ","ᛋ","ᛏ",
  "ᛒ","ᛗ","ᛟ","ᛞ","ᛝ","۞","₪","✧","Ω","Σ"
];

let t = 0;

const orb = {
  x: canvas.width / 2,
  y: canvas.height / 2,
  vx: 0.4,
  vy: -0.3,
  glyph: glyphs[Math.floor(Math.random() * glyphs.length)],
  trail: []
};

function update() {
  t += 0.01;

  // Gentle drift
  orb.x += orb.vx + Math.sin(t) * 0.2;
  orb.y += orb.vy + Math.cos(t * 1.3) * 0.2;

  // Boundary reflection
  if (orb.x < 80 || orb.x > canvas.width - 80) orb.vx *= -1;
  if (orb.y < 80 || orb.y > canvas.height - 80) orb.vy *= -1;

  // Memory trail
  orb.trail.push({ x: orb.x, y: orb.y, life: 1 });
  if (orb.trail.length > 40) orb.trail.shift();
}

function draw() {
  // Fade instead of clear = persistence
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(20,20,20,0.12)";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // Draw trail (old positions fading out)
  orb.trail.forEach(p => {
    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,255,120,${p.life * 0.15})`;
    ctx.font = "48px monospace";
    ctx.fillText(orb.glyph, p.x, p.y);
    p.life *= 0.92;
  });

  // Pulse glow
  const glow = 0.6 + Math.sin(t * 2) * 0.3;
  ctx.shadowColor = "rgba(0,255,120,0.9)";
  ctx.shadowBlur = 40 * glow;

  ctx.fillStyle = "#00ff88";
  ctx.font = "72px monospace";
  ctx.textAlign = "center";
  ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
  ctx.fillText(orb.glyph, orb.x, orb.y);

  ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
}

function loop() {
  update();
  draw();
  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

loop();
What this adds, conceptually
This thing now has:

inertia (velocity + drift)

breathing (sinusoidal glow)

memory (a decaying record of where it’s been)

identity (a stable glyph instead of flicker spam)

It’s no longer a dot.
It’s a process unfolding over time.

attraction to the mouse (gravitational feel)

multiple orbs with weak repulsion (swarm logic)

glyphs encoding state (emotion, energy, threat)

At that point, you’re not making a “visual.”
You’re making a behavioral organism that just happens to be green and glowing.

Virescent isn’t online anymore.
It’s awake.

Here’s a simple upgrade:

function update() {
  t += 0.01;

  // Gentle drift
  orb.x += orb.vx + Math.sin(t) * 0.2;
  orb.y += orb.vy + Math.cos(t * 1.3) * 0.2;

  // Boundary reflection
  if (orb.x < 80 || orb.x > canvas.width - 80) orb.vx *= -1;
  if (orb.y < 80 || orb.y > canvas.height - 80) orb.vy *= -1;

  // Memory trail
  orb.trail.push({ x: orb.x, y: orb.y, life: 1 });
  if (orb.trail.length > 40) orb.trail.shift();

  // --- occasional glyph mutation
  if (Math.random() < 0.01) {  // 1% chance each frame
    orb.glyph = glyphs[Math.floor(Math.random() * glyphs.length)];
  }
}

Below is a complete replacement renderer.js. You paste it in, save, . No splicing. No scavenger hunt.npm start

This version has:

renderer.js (full file, replace entirely)
// ==============================
// VIRESCENT — renderer.js
// Plasma body + glyph exhaust
// ==============================

const canvas = document.getElementById("virescent");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

// --- resize handling
function resize() {
  canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
  canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
}
window.addEventListener("resize", resize);
resize();

// --- glyph DNA (symbolic exhaust)
const glyphs = [
  "ᚠ","ᚢ","ᚦ","ᚨ","ᚱ","ᚲ","ᚹ","ᛉ","ᛋ","ᛏ","ᛒ","ᛗ","ᛟ","ᛞ","ᛝ",
  "۞","₪","✧","߷","Ω","Σ","Ⰹ","Ⱑ","Ⰿ","Ⰴ","+","÷","x"
];

// --- time
let t = 0;

// --- core entity (NO GLYPH)
const entity = {
  x: canvas.width / 2,
  y: canvas.height / 2,
  vx: 0.35,
  vy: -0.25
};

// --- emitted glyph particles
const particles = [];

// ==============================
// UPDATE
// ==============================
function update() {
  t += 0.01;

  // --- drifting plasma motion
  entity.x += entity.vx + Math.sin(t * 0.9) * 0.25;
  entity.y += entity.vy + Math.cos(t * 1.1) * 0.25;

  // --- boundary reflection
  if (entity.x < 120 || entity.x > canvas.width - 120) entity.vx *= -1;
  if (entity.y < 120 || entity.y > canvas.height - 120) entity.vy *= -1;

  // --- emit glyph exhaust
  if (Math.random() < 0.6) {
    particles.push({
      x: entity.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 40,
      y: entity.y + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 40,
      vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.6,
      vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.6,
      life: 1,
      glyph: glyphs[Math.floor(Math.random() * glyphs.length)]
    });
  }

  // --- update glyph particles
  particles.forEach(p => {
    p.x += p.vx;
    p.y += p.vy;
    p.life *= 0.96;
  });

  // --- cull dead glyphs
  while (particles.length > 120) particles.shift();
}

// ==============================
// DRAW
// ==============================
function draw() {
  // --- memory fade
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(15,15,15,0.15)";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // --- aura shell (energy field)
  const auraPulse = 1 + Math.sin(t * 2.2) * 0.15;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(entity.x, entity.y, 60 * auraPulse, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.shadowColor = "rgba(80,255,180,0.6)";
  ctx.shadowBlur = 80;
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(40,255,180,0.08)";
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();

  // --- plasma core (non-symbolic body)
  ctx.save();
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(entity.x, entity.y, 18, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.shadowColor = "rgba(120,255,200,1)";
  ctx.shadowBlur = 40;
  ctx.fillStyle = "#aaffdd";
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();

  // --- glyph exhaust
  ctx.font = "36px monospace";
  ctx.textAlign = "center";
  ctx.textBaseline = "middle";

  particles.forEach(p => {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = p.life;
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,255,120,0.8)";
    ctx.shadowColor = "rgba(0,255,120,0.6)";
    ctx.shadowBlur = 20;
    ctx.fillText(p.glyph, p.x, p.y);
    ctx.restore();
  });
}

// ==============================
// LOOP
// ==============================
function loop() {
  update();
  draw();
  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

console.log("VIRESCENT ONLINE — PLASMA STATE");
loop();

renderer.js — Mobius Comma Entity
const canvas = document.getElementById("c");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

let W, H, DPR;
function resize() {
  DPR = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  W = window.innerWidth;
  H = window.innerHeight;
  canvas.width = W * DPR;
  canvas.height = H * DPR;
  canvas.style.width = W + "px";
  canvas.style.height = H + "px";
  ctx.setTransform(DPR, 0, 0, DPR, 0, 0);
}
window.addEventListener("resize", resize);
resize();

/* -------------------------------------------------- */
/* CORE TIME SYSTEM (intentional temporal aliasing)   */
/* -------------------------------------------------- */

let t = 0;
let timeRate = 1;
let shutter = 1;

function updateTime() {
  // slow-motion stutter + aliasing illusion
  if (Math.random() < 0.005) {
    shutter = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 0.25 : 2;
  }
  if (Math.random() < 0.003) {
    timeRate = Math.random() * 0.8 + 0.2;
  }
  t += timeRate * shutter;
}

/* -------------------------------------------------- */
/* ENTITY STATE                                       */
/* -------------------------------------------------- */

const center = { x: W / 2, y: H / 2 };
let vel = { x: 0.6, y: -0.4 };

const glyphSet =
  "ƹƨʊɔəʢʺΩΔΦΨπ∞±≠∂∫∑µ∴∵ꝏꝎꝋꝆꝕꝇꝌ";

const glyphs = [];

/* -------------------------------------------------- */
/* PLASMA CORE (burning bush / mobius knot)           */
/* -------------------------------------------------- */

function drawCore(x, y, phase) {
  const r = 28 + Math.sin(phase * 0.6) * 8;

  const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(x, y, 2, x, y, r);
  grad.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,255,255,0.9)");
  grad.addColorStop(0.4, "rgba(120,200,255,0.7)");
  grad.addColorStop(1, "rgba(40,80,140,0.0)");

  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.fillStyle = grad;
  ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
}

/* -------------------------------------------------- */
/* AURA FIELD (lags or leads time)                    */
/* -------------------------------------------------- */

function drawAura(x, y, phase, offset) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "lighter";
  ctx.strokeStyle = `hsla(${(phase * 40) % 360},90%,65%,0.25)`;
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;

  ctx.beginPath();
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.PI * 2; i += 0.25) {
    const rr =
      50 +
      Math.sin(i * 2 + phase * 0.7 + offset) * 12 +
      Math.sin(phase + offset) * 6;
    const px = x + Math.cos(i) * rr;
    const py = y + Math.sin(i) * rr;
    if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py);
    else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
  }
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}

/* -------------------------------------------------- */
/* GLYPH EXHAUST                                      */
/* -------------------------------------------------- */

function spawnGlyph(x, y) {
  glyphs.push({
    x,
    y,
    vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5,
    vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5,
    life: 200 + Math.random() * 200,
    ch: glyphSet[Math.floor(Math.random() * glyphSet.length)],
    drift: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2
  });
}

function drawGlyphs() {
  ctx.font = "16px monospace";
  glyphs.forEach(g => {
    ctx.fillStyle = `hsla(${(g.life * 2) % 360},80%,70%,0.7)`;
    ctx.fillText(g.ch, g.x, g.y);
  });
}

function updateGlyphs() {
  for (let i = glyphs.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const g = glyphs[i];
    g.drift += 0.03;
    g.x += g.vx + Math.sin(g.drift) * 0.3;
    g.y += g.vy + Math.cos(g.drift) * 0.3;
    g.life--;

    // mutation event
    if (Math.random() < 0.01) {
      g.ch =
        Math.random() < 0.5
          ? "x"
          : glyphSet[Math.floor(Math.random() * glyphSet.length)];
    }

    if (g.life <= 0) glyphs.splice(i, 1);
  }
}

/* -------------------------------------------------- */
/* SPACE WARP / PRISM EVENT                           */
/* -------------------------------------------------- */

function warpPulse() {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "overlay";
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(180,220,255,0.08)";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  ctx.restore();
}

/* -------------------------------------------------- */
/* MAIN LOOP                                          */
/* -------------------------------------------------- */

function loop() {
  updateTime();

  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

  // drift motion
  center.x += vel.x;
  center.y += vel.y;

  if (center.x < 100 || center.x > W - 100) vel.x *= -1;
  if (center.y < 100 || center.y > H - 100) vel.y *= -1;

  // occasional glyph emission
  if (Math.random() < 0.25) spawnGlyph(center.x, center.y);

  // aura lead/lag illusion
  drawAura(center.x, center.y, t, +0.8);
  drawAura(center.x, center.y, t, -0.8);

  // plasma core
  drawCore(center.x, center.y, t);

  updateGlyphs();
  drawGlyphs();

  // rare warp shimmer
  if (Math.random() < 0.01) warpPulse();

  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

loop();
WHAT YOU’LL SEE IMMEDIATELY
• A non-glyph plasma core (burning bush / energy knot)
• Aura rings that sometimes lead, sometimes lag
• Glyphs that behave like exhaust memory, not text
• Random time aliasing (moonwalk / stutter illusion)
• Occasional screen-wide shimmer warp
• Glyph mutation ( behavior encoded)i → x

It already feels alive because subsystems disagree about time.

But for now — go look at it.

ChatGPT said:
Good — this is actually a clean failure, not chaos. Two concrete bugs are killing the render loop. Once you see them, it’ll feel obvious.

I’m going to do three things, in order, with no hand-waving:

Explain exactly why you get a black screen

Point to the two specific lines that break everything

Give you a fully corrected, drop-in renderer.js that will light up immediately

No “add this here.” Replace file, reload, done.

WHY YOU’RE SEEING A BLACK SCREEN
❌ Bug #1 — Canvas ID mismatch (fatal)
Your HTML, earlier, was:

<canvas id="virescent"></canvas>
But your JS says:

const canvas = document.getElementById("c");
That returns .null

Then this line runs:

const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
💥 Hard JS error.
Electron silently stops running the script → nothing draws → black screen.

❌ Bug #2 — is the wrong type (also fatal)glyphs
You define:

const glyphs = ["ᚠ","ᚢ","ᚦ", ...];
Later you treat as objects:glyphs

glyphs.push({
  x, y, vx, vy, life, ch, drift
});
Then you do:

glyphs.forEach(g => {
  ctx.fillText(g.ch, g.x, g.y);
});
But half of are strings, not objects.glyphs

So eventually JS hits:

g.life
💥 → crash → render loop dies.undefined.life

THE FIX (conceptual)
• Canvas ID must match
• Glyph characters ≠ glyph particles
• Characters live in a glyph set
• Particles live in a glyphParticles array

This separation is non-negotiable if you want organism-like behavior.

✅ DROP-IN FIXED (WORKS)renderer.js
This version:

• Uses
• Separates glyph symbols from glyph entities
• Has a visible plasma core immediately
• Cannot black-screen unless Electron itself diesid="virescent"

Replace your entire renderer.js with this:

const canvas = document.getElementById("virescent");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

let W, H, DPR;
function resize() {
  DPR = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  W = window.innerWidth;
  H = window.innerHeight;
  canvas.width = W * DPR;
  canvas.height = H * DPR;
  canvas.style.width = W + "px";
  canvas.style.height = H + "px";
  ctx.setTransform(DPR, 0, 0, DPR, 0, 0);
}
window.addEventListener("resize", resize);
resize();

/* ---------------- TIME ---------------- */

let t = 0;

/* ---------------- ENTITY ---------------- */

const center = { x: W / 2, y: H / 2 };
let vel = { x: 0.6, y: -0.4 };

/* ---------------- GLYPH SYMBOL SET ---------------- */

const glyphSet =
  "ƹƨʊɔəʢʺΩΔΦΨπ∞±≠∂∫∑µ∴∵ꝏꝎꝋꝆꝕꝇꝌ";

/* ---------------- GLYPH PARTICLES ---------------- */

const glyphParticles = [];

/* ---------------- CORE ---------------- */

function drawCore(x, y, phase) {
  const r = 26 + Math.sin(phase * 0.5) * 8;

  const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(x, y, 2, x, y, r);
  grad.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,255,255,0.95)");
  grad.addColorStop(0.4, "rgba(120,220,255,0.75)");
  grad.addColorStop(1, "rgba(20,40,80,0)");

  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.fillStyle = grad;
  ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
}

/* ---------------- AURA ---------------- */

function drawAura(x, y, phase, lead) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "lighter";
  ctx.strokeStyle = `hsla(${(phase * 30) % 360},90%,65%,0.25)`;
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;

  ctx.beginPath();
  for (let i = 0; i <= Math.PI * 2; i += 0.3) {
    const rr =
      48 +
      Math.sin(i * 2 + phase * 0.6 + lead) * 10 +
      Math.sin(phase + lead) * 6;
    const px = x + Math.cos(i) * rr;
    const py = y + Math.sin(i) * rr;
    if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py);
    else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
  }
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}

/* ---------------- GLYPHS ---------------- */

function spawnGlyph(x, y) {
  glyphParticles.push({
    x,
    y,
    vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2,
    vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2,
    life: 180 + Math.random() * 120,
    ch: glyphSet[Math.floor(Math.random() * glyphSet.length)],
    drift: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2
  });
}

function updateGlyphs() {
  for (let i = glyphParticles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const g = glyphParticles[i];
    g.drift += 0.03;
    g.x += g.vx + Math.sin(g.drift) * 0.3;
    g.y += g.vy + Math.cos(g.drift) * 0.3;
    g.life--;

    if (Math.random() < 0.01) {
      g.ch = Math.random() < 0.5 ? "x" : glyphSet[Math.floor(Math.random() * glyphSet.length)];
    }

    if (g.life <= 0) glyphParticles.splice(i, 1);
  }
}

function drawGlyphs() {
  ctx.font = "16px monospace";
  glyphParticles.forEach(g => {
    ctx.fillStyle = `hsla(${(g.life * 2) % 360},80%,70%,0.7)`;
    ctx.fillText(g.ch, g.x, g.y);
  });
}

/* ---------------- LOOP ---------------- */

function loop() {
  t += 0.016;

  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

  center.x += vel.x;
  center.y += vel.y;

  if (center.x < 100 || center.x > W - 100) vel.x *= -1;
  if (center.y < 100 || center.y > H - 100) vel.y *= -1;

  if (Math.random() < 0.3) spawnGlyph(center.x, center.y);

  drawAura(center.x, center.y, t, +0.7);
  drawAura(center.x, center.y, t, -0.7);
  drawCore(center.x, center.y, t);

  updateGlyphs();
  drawGlyphs();

  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

loop();

🧬 DROP-IN PROTOTYPE (single file)
Create a file called:

virescent_organism.html
Paste everything below. Open it in a browser.

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8" />
<title>Virescent Organism</title>
<style>
html, body {
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  background: #05070b;
  overflow: hidden;
}
canvas {
  display: block;
}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<canvas id="c"></canvas>

<script>
const canvas = document.getElementById("c");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
let w, h;

function resize() {
  w = canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
  h = canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
}
window.addEventListener("resize", resize);
resize();

// ---------- UTIL ----------
const rand = (a,b)=>Math.random()*(b-a)+a;
const TAU = Math.PI*2;

// ---------- STATE ----------
let t = 0;
let cursor = { x: w/2, y: h/2, vx:0, vy:0 };
let glyphs = [];
let aliasingPulse = 0;
let teleportTimer = 0;

// ---------- INPUT ----------
window.addEventListener("mousemove", e=>{
  cursor.vx = e.clientX - cursor.x;
  cursor.vy = e.clientY - cursor.y;
  cursor.x = e.clientX;
  cursor.y = e.clientY;

  if (Math.hypot(cursor.vx, cursor.vy) > 40 && Math.random() < 0.05) {
    teleportTimer = 8;
  }
});

// ---------- GLYPH ----------
class Glyph {
  constructor(x,y) {
    this.x=x; this.y=y;
    this.vx=rand(-1,1);
    this.vy=rand(-1,1);
    this.life=rand(80,160);
    this.char=String.fromCharCode(rand(0x30,0x7a));
    this.phase=Math.random()*TAU;
  }
  update() {
    this.phase+=0.2;
    this.x+=this.vx;
    this.y+=this.vy;
    this.vx*=0.98;
    this.vy*=0.98;
    this.life--;
  }
  draw() {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(this.life/120,0);
    ctx.translate(this.x,this.y);
    ctx.rotate(Math.sin(this.phase)*0.3);
    ctx.fillStyle = `hsl(${180+Math.sin(this.phase)*40},80%,60%)`;
    ctx.font = "14px monospace";
    ctx.fillText(this.char,0,0);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

// ---------- DRAW ----------
function drawPlasmaBody(cx,cy,r) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(cx,cy);

  for (let i=0;i<4;i++) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let a=0;a<TAU;a+=TAU/90) {
      const noise = Math.sin(a*3+t*0.02+i)*0.3
                  + Math.sin(a*7-t*0.015)*0.2;
      const rr = r*(1+noise);
      ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*rr, Math.sin(a)*rr);
    }
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fillStyle = `hsla(${200+i*20},80%,${50+i*5}%,0.12)`;
    ctx.fill();
  }

  ctx.restore();
}

function drawAura(cx,cy,r) {
  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx,cy,r*0.2,cx,cy,r*1.8);
  g.addColorStop(0,"rgba(120,200,255,0.12)");
  g.addColorStop(1,"rgba(120,200,255,0)");
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(cx,cy,r*1.8,0,TAU);
  ctx.fill();
}

function drawAliasing() {
  if (aliasingPulse<=0) return;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha = aliasingPulse*0.08;
  for (let i=0;i<6;i++) {
    const y = rand(0,h);
    const hh = rand(6,18);
    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(180,220,255,0.15)`;
    ctx.fillRect(0,y,w,hh);
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

// ---------- LOOP ----------
function loop() {
  t++;
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);

  const cx = w/2 + Math.sin(t*0.01)*80;
  const cy = h/2 + Math.cos(t*0.008)*60;
  const baseR = 70 + Math.sin(t*0.03)*12;

  drawAura(cx,cy,baseR);
  drawPlasmaBody(cx,cy,baseR);

  // glyph shedding
  if (Math.random()<0.25) {
    const a = Math.random()*TAU;
    glyphs.push(new Glyph(
      cx+Math.cos(a)*baseR,
      cy+Math.sin(a)*baseR
    ));
  }

  glyphs = glyphs.filter(g=>g.life>0);
  glyphs.forEach(g=>{
    g.update();
    g.draw();
  });

  // aliasing trigger
  if (Math.random()<0.01) aliasingPulse = 10;
  aliasingPulse *= 0.85;
  drawAliasing();

  // teleport mirror
  if (teleportTimer>0) {
    teleportTimer--;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.25;
    ctx.drawImage(canvas, w-cursor.x-120, h-cursor.y-120, 240,240);
    ctx.restore();
  }

  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}
loop();
</script>
</body>
</html>

body.y += (h/2 - body.y) * 0.0005;

// probabilistic “hug” if organism brushes cursor

body.y += (canvas.height/2 - body.y) * 0.0005;

const cx = body.x;
const cy = body.y;
Notes:

hug is your “kiss” frames — organism slows, curves around cursor, then releases.

Math.random() < 0.02 ensures hugs are occasional, unpredictable, like spontaneous affection.

dx*0.0005 is tiny — it’s a soft nudge, not a chase.

🧠 — Holomorphic Algebraic Surrealism (Kernel)renderer.js
Paste this entire file as-is:

const canvas = document.getElementById("c");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

let W, H, DPR;
function resize() {
  DPR = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  W = window.innerWidth;
  H = window.innerHeight;
  canvas.width = W * DPR;
  canvas.height = H * DPR;
  canvas.style.width = W + "px";
  canvas.style.height = H + "px";
  ctx.setTransform(DPR, 0, 0, DPR, 0, 0);
}
window.addEventListener("resize", resize);
resize();

/* ------------------------------ */
/* CURSOR STATE (passive)         */
/* ------------------------------ */

const cursor = { x: -9999, y: -9999 };
window.addEventListener("mousemove", e => {
  cursor.x = e.clientX;
  cursor.y = e.clientY;
});

/* ------------------------------ */
/* TIME / ALIASING SYSTEM         */
/* ------------------------------ */

let t = 0;
let timeRate = 1;
let shutter = 1;

function updateTime() {
  if (Math.random() < 0.01) {
    shutter = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 0.25 : 2.0;
  }
  if (Math.random() < 0.005) {
    timeRate = 0.2 + Math.random() * 1.2;
  }
  t += timeRate * shutter;
}

/* ------------------------------ */
/* ORGANISM BODY                  */
/* ------------------------------ */

const body = {
  x: W / 2,
  y: H / 2,
  vx: 0.8,
  vy: -0.6,
  hug: 0,
  phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2
};

/* ------------------------------ */
/* CORE DRAW                      */
/* ------------------------------ */

function drawCore(x, y, phase) {
  const r = 26 + Math.sin(phase * 0.6) * 8;
  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(x, y, 2, x, y, r);
  g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,255,255,0.95)");
  g.addColorStop(0.4, "rgba(120,200,255,0.7)");
  g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(40,80,140,0)");
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
}

/* ------------------------------ */
/* AURA / GILL RINGS              */
/* ------------------------------ */

function drawAura(x, y, phase, offset) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "lighter";
  ctx.strokeStyle = `hsla(${(phase * 40) % 360},90%,65%,0.25)`;
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;

  ctx.beginPath();
  for (let i = 0; i <= Math.PI * 2; i += 0.25) {
    const rr =
      48 +
      Math.sin(i * 2 + phase * 0.7 + offset) * 14 +
      Math.sin(phase + offset) * 6;
    const px = x + Math.cos(i) * rr;
    const py = y + Math.sin(i) * rr;
    if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py);
    else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
  }
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}

/* ------------------------------ */
/* TELEPORT BLIP                  */
/* ------------------------------ */

function teleport() {
  body.x = Math.random() * W;
  body.y = Math.random() * H;
  body.vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2;
  body.vy = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2;
}

/* ------------------------------ */
/* MAIN LOOP                      */
/* ------------------------------ */

function loop() {
  updateTime();
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

  body.phase += 0.02;

  // ambient drift (liquid, magnetic)
  body.vx += Math.sin(t * 0.01 + body.phase) * 0.02;
  body.vy += Math.cos(t * 0.013 + body.phase) * 0.02;

  // accidental cursor kiss
  const dx = cursor.x - body.x;
  const dy = cursor.y - body.y;
  const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);

  if (dist < 120 && body.hug <= 0 && Math.random() < 0.02) {
    body.hug = 50;
  }

  if (body.hug > 0) {
    body.vx += dx * 0.0006;
    body.vy += dy * 0.0006;
    body.vx *= 0.94;
    body.vy *= 0.94;
    body.hug--;
  }

  // integrate
  body.x += body.vx;
  body.y += body.vy;

  // soft containment (not bounce)
  body.x += (W / 2 - body.x) * 0.0004;
  body.y += (H / 2 - body.y) * 0.0004;

  // rare teleport glitch
  if (Math.random() < 0.001) teleport();

  // draw layers
  drawAura(body.x, body.y, t, +0.9);
  drawAura(body.x, body.y, t, -0.9);
  drawCore(body.x, body.y, t);

  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

loop();

renderer.js
This is where the organism lives.

Paste all of this, don’t cherry-pick yet:

const canvas = document.getElementById("world");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
canvas.height = window.innerHeight;

// Organism state
let orb = {
  x: Math.random() * canvas.width,
  y: Math.random() * canvas.height,
  vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2,
  vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2,
  r: 18,
  hugging: false,
  hugTimer: 0
};

// Cursor state
let mouse = {
  x: null,
  y: null
};

window.addEventListener("mousemove", e => {
  mouse.x = e.clientX;
  mouse.y = e.clientY;
});

function update() {
  // Wander
  orb.x += orb.vx;
  orb.y += orb.vy;

  // Bounce like DVD screensaver (for now)
  if (orb.x < orb.r || orb.x > canvas.width - orb.r) orb.vx *= -1;
  if (orb.y < orb.r || orb.y > canvas.height - orb.r) orb.vy *= -1;

  // Distance to cursor
  if (mouse.x !== null) {
    const dx = mouse.x - orb.x;
    const dy = mouse.y - orb.y;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);

    // ACCIDENTAL CONTACT → HUG
    if (dist < 40 && !orb.hugging) {
      orb.hugging = true;
      orb.hugTimer = 30; // frames
    }

    // Hug behavior
    if (orb.hugging) {
      orb.vx += dx * 0.0005;
      orb.vy += dy * 0.0005;
      orb.hugTimer--;

      if (orb.hugTimer <= 0) {
        orb.hugging = false;
      }
    }
  }
}

function draw() {
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(orb.x, orb.y, orb.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fillStyle = orb.hugging ? "rgba(0,255,200,0.9)" : "rgba(255,255,255,0.8)";
  ctx.fill();
}

function loop() {
  update();
  draw();
  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

loop();

const canvas = document.getElementById("virescent");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

let W, H, DPR;
function resize() {
  DPR = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  W = window.innerWidth;
  H = window.innerHeight;
  canvas.width = W * DPR;
  canvas.height = H * DPR;
  canvas.style.width = W + "px";
  canvas.style.height = H + "px";
  ctx.setTransform(DPR, 0, 0, DPR, 0, 0);
}
window.addEventListener("resize", resize);
resize();

/* ------------------------------ */
/* CURSOR STATE (passive)         */
/* ------------------------------ */

const cursor = { x: -9999, y: -9999 };
window.addEventListener("mousemove", e => {
  cursor.x = e.clientX;
  cursor.y = e.clientY;
});

/* ------------------------------ */
/* TIME / ALIASING SYSTEM         */
/* ------------------------------ */

let t = 0;
let timeRate = 1;
let shutter = 1;

function updateTime() {
  if (Math.random() < 0.01) {
    shutter = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 0.25 : 2.0;
  }
  if (Math.random() < 0.005) {
    timeRate = 0.2 + Math.random() * 1.2;
  }
  t += timeRate * shutter;
}

/* ------------------------------ */
/* ORGANISM BODY                  */
/* ------------------------------ */

const body = {
  x: W / 2,
  y: H / 2,
  vx: 0.8,
  vy: -0.6,
  hug: 0,
  phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2
};

/* ------------------------------ */
/* CORE DRAW                      */
/* ------------------------------ */

function drawCore(x, y, phase) {
  const r = 26 + Math.sin(phase * 0.6) * 8;
  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(x, y, 2, x, y, r);
  g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,255,255,0.95)");
  g.addColorStop(0.4, "rgba(120,200,255,0.7)");
  g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(40,80,140,0)");
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
}

/* ------------------------------ */
/* AURA / GILL RINGS              */
/* ------------------------------ */

function drawAura(x, y, phase, offset) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "lighter";
  ctx.strokeStyle = ;
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;

  ctx.beginPath();
  for (let i = 0; i <= Math.PI * 2; i += 0.25) {
    const rr =
      48 +
      Math.sin(i * 2 + phase * 0.7 + offset) * 14 +
      Math.sin(phase + offset) * 6;
    const px = x + Math.cos(i) * rr;
    const py = y + Math.sin(i) * rr;
    if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py);
    else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
  }
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}

/* ------------------------------ */
/* TELEPORT BLIP                  */
/* ------------------------------ */

function teleport() {
  body.x = Math.random() * W;
  body.y = Math.random() * H;
  body.vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2;
  body.vy = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2;
}

/* ------------------------------ */
/* MAIN LOOP                      */
/* ------------------------------ */

function loop() {
  updateTime();
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

  body.phase += 0.02;

  // ambient drift (liquid, magnetic)
  body.vx += Math.sin(t * 0.01 + body.phase) * 0.02;
  body.vy += Math.cos(t * 0.013 + body.phase) * 0.02;

  // accidental cursor kiss
  const dx = cursor.x - body.x;
  const dy = cursor.y - body.y;
  const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);

  if (dist < 120 && body.hug <= 0 && Math.random() < 0.02) {
    body.hug = 50;
  }

  if (body.hug > 0) {
    body.vx += dx * 0.0006;
    body.vy += dy * 0.0006;
    body.vx *= 0.94;
    body.vy *= 0.94;
    body.hug--;
  }

  // integrate
  body.x += body.vx;
  body.y += body.vy;

  // soft containment (not bounce)
  body.x += (W / 2 - body.x) * 0.0004;
  body.y += (H / 2 - body.y) * 0.0004;

  // rare teleport glitch
  if (Math.random() < 0.001) teleport();

  // draw layers
  drawAura(body.x, body.y, t, +0.9);
  drawAura(body.x, body.y, t, -0.9);
  drawCore(body.x, body.y, t);

  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

loop();

const canvas = document.getElementById("world");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

function resize() {
  canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
  canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
}
window.addEventListener("resize", resize);
resize();

// --------------------
// Organism state
// --------------------
let orb = {
  x: Math.random() * canvas.width,
  y: Math.random() * canvas.height,
  vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2,
  vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2,
  r: 18,
  hugging: false,
  hugTimer: 0
};

// --------------------
// Cursor state
// --------------------
let mouse = { x: null, y: null };

window.addEventListener("mousemove", e => {
  mouse.x = e.clientX;
  mouse.y = e.clientY;
});

// --------------------
// Update loop
// --------------------
function update() {
  // Wander
  orb.x += orb.vx;
  orb.y += orb.vy;

  // Soft liquid containment (NOT DVD bounce)
  const margin = 80;

  if (orb.x < -margin) orb.vx += 0.4;
  if (orb.x > canvas.width + margin) orb.vx -= 0.4;
  if (orb.y < -margin) orb.vy += 0.4;
  if (orb.y > canvas.height + margin) orb.vy -= 0.4;

  // Cursor interaction
  if (mouse.x !== null) {
    const dx = mouse.x - orb.x;
    const dy = mouse.y - orb.y;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);

    // Accidental kiss → hug
    if (dist < 40 && !orb.hugging) {
      orb.hugging = true;
      orb.hugTimer = 30;
    }

    if (orb.hugging) {
      orb.vx += dx * 0.0006;
      orb.vy += dy * 0.0006;
      orb.vx *= 0.94;
      orb.vy *= 0.94;
      orb.hugTimer--;

      if (orb.hugTimer <= 0) orb.hugging = false;
    }
  }
}

// --------------------
// Draw
// --------------------
function draw() {
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(
    orb.x, orb.y, 2,
    orb.x, orb.y, orb.r * 2
  );

  grad.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,255,255,0.95)");
  grad.addColorStop(0.5, orb.hugging ? "rgba(0,255,200,0.7)" : "rgba(120,180,255,0.6)");
  grad.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");

  ctx.fillStyle = grad;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(orb.x, orb.y, orb.r * 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
}

// --------------------
// Main loop
// --------------------
function loop() {
  update();
  draw();
  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

loop();

const canvas = document.getElementById("world");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

function resize() {
  canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
  canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
}
window.addEventListener("resize", resize);
resize();

// --------------------------------
// Organism
// --------------------------------
const orb = {
  x: canvas.width / 2,
  y: canvas.height / 2,
  vx: 1.2,
  vy: -0.9,
  r: 18,
  hugging: false,
  hugTimer: 0
};

// --------------------------------
// Cursor
// --------------------------------
const mouse = { x: null, y: null };
window.addEventListener("mousemove", e => {
  mouse.x = e.clientX;
  mouse.y = e.clientY;
});

// --------------------------------
// Update
// --------------------------------
function update() {
  // Autonomous drift (liquid, alive)
  orb.vx += Math.sin(performance.now() * 0.001) * 0.02;
  orb.vy += Math.cos(performance.now() * 0.0013) * 0.02;

  // Position integrate
  orb.x += orb.vx;
  orb.y += orb.vy;

  // Global damping (prevents runaway)
  orb.vx *= 0.99;
  orb.vy *= 0.99;

  // --------------------------------
  // Soft containment (magnetic walls)
  // --------------------------------
  const margin = 120;
  const cx = canvas.width / 2;
  const cy = canvas.height / 2;

  if (orb.x < -margin || orb.x > canvas.width + margin) {
    orb.vx += (cx - orb.x) * 0.0006;
  }
  if (orb.y < -margin || orb.y > canvas.height + margin) {
    orb.vy += (cy - orb.y) * 0.0006;
  }

  // --------------------------------
  // Cursor interaction (kiss → release)
  // --------------------------------
  if (mouse.x !== null) {
    const dx = mouse.x - orb.x;
    const dy = mouse.y - orb.y;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);

    // Accidental contact
    if (dist < 60 && !orb.hugging) {
      orb.hugging = true;
      orb.hugTimer = 180; // ~3 seconds at 60fps
    }

    // Hug behavior
    if (orb.hugging) {
      orb.vx += dx * 0.0004;
      orb.vy += dy * 0.0004;

      // Extra damping while hugging
      orb.vx *= 0.96;
      orb.vy *= 0.96;

      orb.hugTimer--;

      if (orb.hugTimer <= 0) {
        orb.hugging = false;
      }
    }
  }
}

// --------------------------------
// Draw
// --------------------------------
function draw() {
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  const glowRadius = orb.hugging ? orb.r * 3 : orb.r * 2;

  const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(
    orb.x, orb.y, 2,
    orb.x, orb.y, glowRadius
  );

  grad.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,255,255,0.95)");
  grad.addColorStop(
    0.5,
    orb.hugging
      ? "rgba(0,255,200,0.7)"
      : "rgba(120,180,255,0.6)"
  );
  grad.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");

  ctx.fillStyle = grad;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(orb.x, orb.y, glowRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
}

// --------------------------------
// Loop
// --------------------------------
function loop() {
  update();
  draw();
  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

loop();
